```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Add the Silent Storm Confetti Blowers - T item to Quoter.

This will create the missing item in Quoter to match Pipedrive.
"""

import os
import requests
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from utils.logger import logger

load_dotenv()

# API Configuration
CLIENT_ID = os.getenv("QUOTER_API_KEY")
CLIENT_SECRET = os.getenv("QUOTER_CLIENT_SECRET")

# Item details from Pipedrive
ITEM_DETAILS = {
    "name": "Silent Storm Confetti Blowers - T",
    "code": "CNF-BLW-T",
    "description": "Silent Storm Confetti Blowers - Tall Version",
    "price": 12.99,  # $12.99
    "pipedrive_id": 757
}

def get_access_token():
    """Get OAuth access token from Quoter API."""
    if not CLIENT_ID or not CLIENT_SECRET:
        logger.error("CLIENT_ID or CLIENT_SECRET not found in environment variables")
        return None
    
    auth_url = "https://api.quoter.com/v1/auth/oauth/authorize"
    auth_data = {
        "client_id": CLIENT_ID,
        "client_secret": CLIENT_SECRET,
        "grant_type": "client_credentials"
    }
    
    try:
        logger.info("Getting OAuth access token from Quoter...")
        response = requests.post(auth_url, json=auth_data, timeout=10)
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            data = response.json()
            access_token = data.get("access_token")
            if access_token:
                logger.info("✅ Successfully obtained OAuth access token")
                return access_token
            else:
                logger.error("❌ No access_token in response")
                return None
        else:
            logger.error(f"❌ OAuth authentication failed: {response.status_code} - {response.text}")
            return None
            
    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ Error getting OAuth token: {e}")
        return None

def create_quoter_item(item_details):
    """Create a new item in Quoter."""
    access_token = get_access_token()
    if not access_token:
        return False
    
    headers = {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {access_token}",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }
    
    # Prepare item data for Quoter
    item_data = {
        "name": item_details["name"],
        "code": item_details["code"],
        "description": item_details["description"],
        "price": item_details["price"],
        "sku": str(item_details["pipedrive_id"]),  # Map Pipedrive ID to Quoter SKU
        "active": True
    }
    
    try:
        logger.info(f"Creating new item in Quoter: {item_details['name']}")
        
        response = requests.post(
            "https://api.quoter.com/v1/items",
            headers=headers,
            json=item_data,
            timeout=10
        )
        
        if response.status_code in [200, 201]:
            data = response.json()
            item_id = data.get("data", {}).get("id")
            logger.info(f"✅ Successfully created item in Quoter with ID: {item_id}")
            return True
        else:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed to create item: {response.status_code} - {response.text}")
            return False
            
    except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
        logger.error(f"❌ Error creating item: {e}")
        return False

def main():
    """Main function to add the Silent Storm Confetti Blowers - T item."""
    logger.info("➕ Adding Silent Storm Confetti Blowers - T to Quoter...")
    
    print("\n" + "="*60)
    print("➕ ADDING SILENT STORM CONFETTI BLOWERS - T TO QUOTER")
    print("="*60)
    
    print(f"Item Name: {ITEM_DETAILS['name']}")
    print(f"Code: {ITEM_DETAILS['code']}")
    print(f"Description: {ITEM_DETAILS['description']}")
    print(f"Price: ${ITEM_DETAILS['price']}")
    print(f"SKU (Pipedrive ID): {ITEM_DETAILS['pipedrive_id']}")
    
    print(f"\nCreating item in Quoter...")
    
    if create_quoter_item(ITEM_DETAILS):
        print("✅ Item created successfully!")
        print("\n🎉 Silent Storm Confetti Blowers - T has been added to Quoter!")
        print("The sync should now be complete with all items matched.")
    else:
        print("❌ Failed to create item")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
